

===== uep-diagnostics.js =====

--- version @ 6 ---
const VERSION = '0.78.6';
const STORAGE_KEY = 'uep.runtimeDiagnostics.v1';

const state = {
  version: VERSION,
  startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  errors: [],
  warnings: [],
  checks: {},
};

function safeText(value) {
  return String(value ?? '').replace(/[<>]/g, '');
}

function pushIssue(kind, issue) {
  const item = { time: new Date().toISOString(), ...issue };
  const bucket = kind === 'error' ? state.errors : state.warnings;
  const signature = `${item.type}|${item.message}|${item.source || ''}|${item.line || ''}`;
  if (!bucket.some((entry) => `${entry.type}|${entry.message}|${entry.source || ''}|${entry.line || ''}` === signature)) {
    bucket.push(item);
    if (bucket.length > 100) bucket.shift();
  }
  persist();
  updateBadge();
}

function persist() {
  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state)); } catch (_) { /* ignore */ }
}

function checkDom() {
  const allWithId = [...document.querySelectorAll('[id]')];
  const counts = new Map();
  allWithId.forEach((node) => counts.set(node.id, (counts.get(node.id) || 0) + 1));
  const duplicateIds = [...counts.entries()].filter(([, count]) => count > 1).map(([id, count]) => ({ id, count }));

  const pages = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-page]')]
    .map((node) => node.dataset.page)
    .filter(Boolean);
  const pageNames = [...new Set(pages)];

  const emptyButtons = [...document.querySelectorAll('button')].filter((button) => {
    if (button.disabled || button.dataset.page || button.id) return false;
    return !button.getAttribute('onclick') && !button.closest('form');
  }).map((button) => butt

--- update @ 2636 ---
lementCount: document.querySelectorAll('*').length,
    idCount: allWithId.length,
    duplicateIds,
    pageNames,
    anonymousButtonCandidates: emptyButtons,
  };

  duplicateIds.forEach(({ id, count }) => pushIssue('error', {
    type: 'duplicate-dom-id',
    message: `중복 DOM ID: ${id} (${count}개)`,
  }));
  persist();
}

function checkAssets() {
  const sheets = [...document.styleSheets];
  const failedStylesheets = sheets.filter((sheet) => !sheet.href && sheet.ownerNode?.tagName === 'LINK')
    .map((sheet) => sheet.ownerNode?.getAttribute('href'));
  state.checks.assets = {
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    stylesheetCount: sheets.length,
    failedStylesheets,
    scriptCount: document.scripts.length,
  };
  failedStylesheets.forEach((href) => pushIssue('error', {
    type: 'stylesheet-load', message: `스타일시트 로드 실패: ${href || '알 수 없음'}`,
  }));
  persist();
}

function updateBadge() {
  const button = document.getElementById('diagnosticsButton');
  if (!button) return;
  const count = state.errors.length + state.warnings.length;
  let badge = button.querySelector('em');
  if (count > 0) {
    if (!badge) { badge = document.createElement('em'); button.appendChild(badge); }
    badge.textContent = String(count);
    button.title = `시스템 진단: 오류 ${state.errors.length}, 경고 ${state.warnings.length}`;
  } else if (badge) {
    badge.remove();
  }
}

function statusLabel() {
  if (state.errors.length) return '점검 필요';
  if (state.warnings.length) return '주의';
  return '정상';
}

function renderDiagnostics() {
  checkDom();
  checkAssets();
  const old = document.getElementById('uepDiagnosticsModal');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const modal = document.createElement('div');
  modal.id = 'uepDiagnosticsModal';
  modal.className = 'uep-diagnostics-backdrop';
  const issues = [...state.errors.map((x) => ({ ...x, level: '오류' })), ...state.warnings.map((x) => ({ ...x, level: '경고' }))];
  const issueHtml = issues.length
    ? issues.slice(-30).reverse().map((item) => `<tr><td>${safeText(item.level)}</td><td>${safeText(item.type)}</td><td>${safeText(item.message)}</td><td>${safeText(item.source || '')}${item.line ? `:${item.line}` : ''}</td></tr>`).join('')
    : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="diag-empty">현재 수집된 오류와 경고가 없습니다.</td></tr>';

  modal.innerHTML = `
    <section class="uep-diagnostics-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="UEP 시스템 진단">
      <header><div><small>UEP v${VERSION}</small><h2>시스템 진단</h2></div><button type="button" id="diagnosticsClos

window.addEventListener('error', (event) => {
  pushIssue('error', {
    type: 'javascript-error',
    message: event.message || '알 수 없는 JavaScript 오류',
    source: event.filename || '', line: event.lineno || '', column: event.colno || '',
  });
}, true);

window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (event) => {
  const reason = event.reason;
  pushIssue('error', {
    type: 'unhandled-promise',
    message: reason?.message || String(reason || '처리되지 않은 Promise 오류'),
    source: reason?.stack || '',
  });
});

function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

    const needsFull=!readonlyCache||!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS;
    const jitter=needsFull?(2000+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000)):(500+Math.floor(Math.random()*2500));
    setTimeout(async()=>{
      const ok=needsFull
        ? await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true})
        : await refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
      if(ok){
        updateTopSyncStatus();
        if(currentRoleId()==='admin') migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce().catch(()=>{});
      }
    },jitter);
  }
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok && readonlyCache) await migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce();
  updateDormOutingCount();
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  // 여기서는 백그라운드에서 최신 계정 유효성을 재검증한다.
  // 자동로그인 세션은 이미 화면을 열었으므로 네트워크 지연이 시작 화면을 막지 않는다.
  await Promise.race([
    initializeUserSessionGate(),
    new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        /* 0.81.20: local remembered state never bypasses School Read session verification */
      }catch{}
      resolve(true);
    },1800))
  ]);
  if(authSessionReady && !resumedRememberedSession) navigate(state.activePage || "dashboard");
  if(false && !googleConnectionStatus?.ok && currentRoleId()==="admin"){
    // 인증정보가 없거나 복호화/유효성 검증에 실패한 경우에는 기존 setupCompleted 값과 무관하게
    // 데이터 연결 단계에서 멈춘다. 학생정보 화면을 먼저 열어 권한부여를 건너뛰지 않는다.
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(3),50);
  }else if(!state.settings.setupCompleted){
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(0),50);
  }else{
    setSetupWizardLock(false);
  }
}

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    readonlyAutoRefreshCycle+=1;
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS||readonlyAutoRefreshCycle%6===0;
    if(needsFull){
      const jitter=Math.floor(Math.random()*45000);
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden)refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});},jitter);
      return;
    }
    refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

async function loadNeisDashboard(force = false, requestedDate = "") {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard) {
    neisError = "이 실행환경에서는 NEIS 연결 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return;
  }
  if (neisLoading) return;
  neisLoading = true;
  if (state.activePage === "dashboard" || state.activePage === "timetable" || state.activePage === "settings") render(state.activePage);
  const options = {
    schoolName: state.settings.neis.schoolName,
    officeCode: state.settings.neis.officeCode,
    schoolCode: state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
    grade: state.settings.neis.grade,
    classNo: state.settings.neis.classNo,
    date: requestedDate || dateKey(new Date()),
    useApiKey: Boolean(state.settings.neis.useApiKey),
    comciganSchoolCode: state.settings.neis.comciganSchoolCode || "69092",
    force,
  };
  try {
    const result = await window.schoolBoard.loadNeisDashboard(options);
    if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason || "NEIS 자료를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
    neisData = result;
    neisError = result.offline ? result.staleReason || "실시간 연결 실패로 마지막 저장자료를 표시합니다." : "";
    if (result.school) {
      state.settings.neis.schoolName = result.school.schoolName || state.settings.neis.schoolName;
      state.settings.neis.officeCode = result.school.officeCode || state.settings.neis.officeCode;
      state.settings.neis.schoolCode = result.school.schoolCode || state.settings.neis.schoolCode;
      await save();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    neisError = error?.message || String(error);
    if (!neisData?.ok) neisData = null;
  } finally {
    neisLoading = false;
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
  }
}

function canConfigureSensitivePassword(){
  // __UEP_08160_SENSITIVE_CONFIG_ROLE__
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  return role==='admin'||role==='grade_head';
}
async function setSensitivePasswordFlow(){
  if(!canConfigureSensitivePassword())return toast('관리자·학년부장만 민감정보 비밀번호를 설정·변경할 수 있습니다.');
  const configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured,kind:'subject'});
  if(!result)return;
  if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
    return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
  }
  const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
  localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));
  lockSensitiveSession();
  toast('민감정보 열람 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');
  if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
}
async function unlockSensitiveInfo(){
  if(sensitiveSessionOpen())return true;
  if(!sensitivePasswordConfigured()){
    toast('설정 → 사용자·보안에서 먼저 비밀번호를 설정해 주세요.');
    return false;
  }
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'unlock',configured:true});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(await sensitiveHash(result.first)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
    toast('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
    return false;
  }
  uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil=Date.now()+10*60*1000;
  return true;
}

const UEP_BOARD_DB_URL="https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1KStE1tJq6LTA8KR8fe56r7OxO1k9Ae8-lIfWw9d4wng/edit";

async function refreshElectronicBoardStatus(){
  const status=document.getElementById("uepBoardApiStatus");
  const detail=document.getElementById("uepBoardApiDetail");
  const time=document.getElementById("uepBoardApiTime");
  if(status){status.textContent="연결 확인 중"; status.dataset.state="loading";}
  if(detail)detail.textContent="UEP Board API 응답을 확인하고 있습니다.";
  try{
    const response=await fetch(UEP_BOARD_API_URL,{method:"GET",cache:"no-store"});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error("HTTP "+response.status);
    const data=await response.json();
    if(!data||data.ok!==true)throw new Error((data&&data.error)||"INVALID_BOARD_API_RESPONSE");
    if(status){status.textContent="연결 정상"; status.dataset.state="ok";}
    if(detail)detail.textContent="UEP Board API "+(data.version?"v"+data.version+" ":"")+"· 실시간 연결됨";
    if(time)time.textContent=data.now?"서버 시각 "+data.now:"";
  }catch(error){
    if(status){status.textContent="연결 확인 필요"; status.dataset.state="error";}
    if(detail)detail.textContent="UEP Board API에 연결하지 못했습니다. 네트워크 또는 API 배포 상태를 확인해 주세요.";
    if(time)time.textContent=String(error&&error.message?error.message:error||"");
  }
}

function calendarEventIsPending(event) {
  return /예약|예정|미정|미기재|검토|대기/.test(String(event?.status || ""));
}

function calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event) {
  const target = String(event?.target || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const detail = String(event?.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\(?1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]?\s*2?\s*학년부[^)]*\)?/g, " ");
  if (/학부모/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
  if (/교사|교직원/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
  const text = target || detail;
  return /(?:^|[^0-9])1\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]\s*2\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /1\s*[~-]\s*3\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /전교생|전체\s*학생/.test(text);
}

scapeHtml(r.status||'확인')}</em></div>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-inline">학사외출 이력이 없습니다.</div>';

function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
  const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
  try{
    const meta=JSON.parse(source);
    const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
    const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
    const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
    if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
  }catch{}
  return source;
}
function inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row={}){
  const reviewed=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.reviewText||'');
  if(reviewed)return reviewed;
  const structured=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.normalizedText||'');
  if(structured&&!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(structured))return structured;
  if(structured&&structured!==String(row.normalizedText||'').trim())return structured;
  return String(row.rawText||row.text||'').trim();
}

}

function selectionOutputRows(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const errors=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
  const subjects=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjects||readonlyCache?.studentSubjects||[];
  return students.map(student=>{
    const studentErrors=errors.filter(error=>
      String(error.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(error.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    const studentSubjects=subjects.filter(row=>
      String(row.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(row.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    return {student,errors:studentErrors,subjects:studentSubjects,status:studentErrors.length?"상담 필요":"정상"};
  }).filter(row=>{
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="error")return row.errors.length>0;
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="normal")return row.errors.length===0;
    return true;
  });
}

function uep08125ValidTeacherName(value){
  const v=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').trim();
  if(!v)return '';
  if(/^(?:\?+|사용자|담당교사|unknown|null|undefined)$/i.test(v))return '';
  return v;
}
function uep08125PickTeacherName(...values){for(const value of values){const v=uep08125ValidTeacherName(value);if(v)return v;}return '';}

function uep08125ValidTeacherName(value){
  const v=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').trim();
  if(!v)return '';
  if(/^(?:\?+|사용자|담당교사|unknown|null|undefined)$/i.test(v))return '';
  return v;
}
function uep08125PickTeacherName(...values){for(const value of values){const v=uep08125ValidTeacherName(value);if(v)return v;}return '';}

async function uep08250RefreshStage(stage){
  if(stage==='admission'&&uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.admission='ready';uep08250Emit();return true;}
  if(UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE){await UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE;return stage!=='admission'||uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();}
  UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='loading';uep08250Emit();
  UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE=(async()=>{
    try{
      const r=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();
      if(!r?.ok)throw new Error(r?.reason||'UEP 자료 동기화 실패');
      const c=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();
      if(!c?.ok||!c.data)throw new Error(c?.reason||'UEP 캐시를 읽지 못했습니다.');
      readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(c.data);googleConnectionError='';updateTopSyncStatus();
      UEP_08250_LOAD.academic=uep08292AcademicRowsReady()?'ready':'error';
      UEP_08250_LOAD.admission=uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()?'ready':'error';
      return true;
    }catch(e){
      if(UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]==='loading')UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='error';
      googleConnectionError=e?.message||String(e);return false;
    }finally{UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE=null;uep08250Emit();}
  })();
  await UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE;
  return stage!=='admission'||uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();
}
async function uep08250StartBackgroundStages(){
  if(UEP_08250_LOAD.running)return;
  UEP_08250_LOAD.running=true;uep08250Emit();
  try{
    if(uep08292AcademicRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.academic='ready';uep08250Emit();}
    else await uep08250RefreshStage('academic');
    if(uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.admission='ready';uep08250Emit();}
    else await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');
  }finally{UEP_08250_LOAD.running=false;uep08250Emit();}
}

  await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');
  return uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();
}

const PRIVACY_MODE_STORE='uep-privacy-demo-mode-v1';
function privacyModeEnabled(){try{return localStorage.getItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE)==='1';}catch{return false;}}
function applyPrivacyMode(){
  const enabled=privacyModeEnabled();
  document.body.classList.toggle('privacy-demo-mode',enabled);
  const button=document.getElementById('privacyModeButton');
  if(button){
    button.classList.toggle('active',enabled);
    const b=button.querySelector('b'); if(b)b.textContent=enabled?'개인정보 보호 중':'개인정보 가리기';
    button.title=enabled?'개인정보 보호 모드 해제':'학생·연수 시연용 개인정보 가리기';
  }
  const signal=document.getElementById('studentSignalButton');
  if(signal&&enabled)signal.title='선택 학생 정보 보호 중';
  else if(signal)updateStudentSignalIndicator();
}
function togglePrivacyMode(){
  const next=!privacyModeEnabled();
  try{localStorage.setItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE,next?'1':'0');}catch{}
  applyPrivacyMode();
  toast(next?'개인정보 보호 모드를 켰습니다. 기능은 유지하고 주요 개인정보만 가립니다.':'개인정보 보호 모드를 해제했습니다.');
}

function accessAccountRole(account){
  if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
  const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
  if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
  if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
  if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
  if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
  if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
  return '교과담당';
}

  const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];

emove(); layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=close); layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};

    layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-user]').forEach(row=>{

      });
      row.querySelector('[data-access-save]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{

        if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'권한을 저장하지 못했습니다.');

      });
    });
  }
}

    {name:'UEP 표준 구글시트',ok:Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length&&!googleConnectionError),note:readonlyCache?.students?.length?`${readonlyCache.students.length}명 학생 인식`:'연결 확인 필요'},
    {name:'사용자 계정 승인표',ok:Boolean((readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).length),note:`${(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).filter(a=>a.active!==false).length}명 승인`},
    {name:'현재 사용자 권한',ok:Boolean(account&&account.active!==false),note:account?`${account.name} · ${account.admin==='Y'?'관리자':account.role||'교과담당'}`:'계정표 일치 확인 필요'},
    {name:'권한별 조회범위',ok:Boolean(currentTeacherAccessScope()?.label),note:currentTeacherAccessScope()?.label||'확인 필요'},
    {name:'업데이트 정보',ok:Boolean(state.settings.updateManifestUrl),note:state.settings.updateManifestUrl?'업데이트 확인 주소 등록':'manifest 주소 등록 필요'},
    {name:'Google API 보호',ok:true,note:'120초 캐시 · 429 재시도 · 순차 batch 조회'},
    {name:'런타임 진단',ok:true,note:'오류·중복 DOM·리소스 점검 활성'}
  ];
}

function settingsTabsMarkup(){
  const tabs=[
    ["basic","01 기본·연결"],
    ["security","02 사용자·보안"],
    ["operations","03 알림·운영"],
    ["system","04 시스템 관리"]
  ];
  return `<div class="settings-tabs settings-final-tabs">${tabs.map(([id,label])=>`<button class="${settingsPanel===id?'active':''}" data-settings-panel="${id}">${label}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

===== tools/UEP-DataProcessor-v1.2.3-daily-dedupe-operational-rules-fullset.js =====

--- version @ 610 ---
/**
 * [UEP] 2026 운호고 데이터 처리시트
 * 학사외출 중앙 처리 엔진
 *
 * 설치 위치:
 * [UEP] 2026 운호고 데이터 처리시트 → 확장 프로그램 → Apps Script
 *
 * 최초 실행:
 * installUepDormOutingProcessor()
 *
 * 운영 원칙:
 * 구글폼 응답 → 데이터처리시트 정규화/일자별 영구원장 → UEP 직접 조회
 * 기본정보 연결시트에는 학사외출 데이터를 다시 쓰지 않습니다.
 * v1.2.3: 영구원장 구버전 중복 제거 + 실제 입퇴소 변경/개인신청 규칙 확장.
 * 학생마스터/학사생마스터는 학생 식별을 위한 읽기 전용 참조만 사용합니다.
 */

const UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR = Object.freeze({
  MENU: 'UEP 데이터 처리',
  CONFIG_SHEET: '00_처리설정',
  NORMALIZED_SHEET: '01_학사외출_정규화',
  DAILY_SHEET: '02_학사외출_일자별',
  LOG_SHEET: '90_처리로그',
  ERROR_SHEET: '91_오류검토',
  RETRY_SHEET: '92_재처리대기',
  TZ: 'Asia/Seoul',
  VERSION: '1.2.4',
  AUTO_HANDLER: 'runUepDormOutingProcessorAuto',
  OPEN_HANDLER: 'onOpen',
  COLLECTION_SPREADSHEET_ID: '1K-Me8Pm8T2d5fGHoqSPEAu4RXozETUQ-N5pZVxjs8Hw',
  COLLECTION_SHEET: '01_학사외출',
  RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID: '1hRNvoUmNISEftmrMvjeo6CfAt9BJx3yjeHWAiT1M77s',
  RULES_SHEET: '40_학사외출규칙',
  MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT: '1bphoIQ11E2qsc3ksOqrXuO7xqS4CFyH8Dagbld7A1hg',
  HOME_CATEGORIES: ['학원 후 귀가', '병원 후 귀가', '귀가'],
  RETURN_CATEGORIES: [
    '방과후',
    '학교 프로그램 참석',
    '야간 모의고사 수업',
    '공동교육과정 수업 참여',
    '학원 외출',
    '병원 외출',
    '기타 외출',
  ],
});

function onOpen() {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi()
    .createMenu(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU)
    .addItem('① 학사외출 전체 처리', 'runUepDormOutingProcessor')
    .addItem('② 정규화 원장 갱신', 'buildDormOutingNormalizedOnly')
    .addItem('③ 일자별·규칙판정 갱신', 'buildDormOutingDailyOnly')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('④ 자동 처리 트리거 설치', 'installUepDormOutingAutoTrigger')
    .addItem('⑤ 처리 상태 확인', 'showUepDormOutingProcessorStatus')
    .addItem('⑥ 오늘 외출현황 메일 점검', 'sendTodayDormOutingMailIfNeeded_')
    .addToUi();
}

function installUepDormOutingProcessor() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  validateRequiredSheets_();
  setConfigValue_('SCRIPT_STATUS', 'INSTALLED');
  setConfigValue_('SCRIPT_VERSION', UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.VERSION);
  setConfigValue_('PROCESS_PATH', 'FORM→NORMALIZED→DAILY→UEP_DIRECT');
  setConfigValue_('LEGACY_TARGET_WRITE', 'DISABLED');
  configureSourceFormSafety_();
  installUepDormOutingAutoTrigger();
  runUepDormOutingProcessor();
  ss.toast(
    '학사외출 중앙 처리 엔진 설치가 완료되었습니다.',
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU,
    8
  );

function installUepDormOutingProcessor() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  validateRequiredSheets_();
  setConfigValue_('SCRIPT_STATUS', 'INSTALLED');
  setConfigValue_('SCRIPT_VERSION', UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.VERSION);
  setConfigValue_('PROCESS_PATH', 'FORM→NORMALIZED→DAILY→UEP_DIRECT');
  setConfigValue_('LEGACY_TARGET_WRITE', 'DISABLED');
  configureSourceFormSafety_();
  installUepDormOutingAutoTrigger();
  runUepDormOutingProcessor();
  ss.toast(
    '학사외출 중앙 처리 엔진 설치가 완료되었습니다.',
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU,
    8
  );
}

function installUepDormOutingAutoTrigger() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const interval = Number(config.AUTO_INTERVAL_MINUTES || 5);
  const validIntervals = [1, 5, 10, 15, 30];
  const minutes = validIntervals.includes(interval) ? interval : 5;

  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach((trigger) => {
    if (trigger.getHandlerFunction() === UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER) {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);
    }
  });

  ScriptApp.newTrigger(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .everyMinutes(minutes)
    .create();

  setConfigValue_('AUTO_TRIGGER_STATUS', `INSTALLED_${minutes}MIN`);
  SpreadsheetApp.getActive().toast(
    `${minutes}분 간격 자동 처리 트리거를 설치했습니다.`,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU,
    7
  );
}

function runUepDormOutingProcessorAuto() {
  const result = processDormOutingPipeline_({ interactive: false });
  if (result && result.ok) sendTodayDormOutingMailIfNeeded_();
}

function runUepDormOutingProcessor() {
  return processDormOutingPipeline_({ interactive: true });
}

  const started = new Date();
  try {
    const context = loadDormOutingConte

  ScriptApp.newTrigger(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .everyMinutes(minutes)
    .create();

  setConfigValue_('AUTO_TRIGGER_STATUS', `INSTALLED_${minutes}MIN`);
  SpreadsheetApp.getActive().toast(
    `${minutes}분 간격 자동 처리 트리거를 설치했습니다.`,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU,
    7
  );
}

function runUepDormOutingProcessorAuto() {
  const result = processDormOutingPipeline_({ interactive: false });
  if (result && result.ok) sendTodayDormOutingMailIfNeeded_();
}

}

function getConfig_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.CONFIG_SHEET);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('00_처리설정 탭이 없습니다.');

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 3) return {};

  // 표준 구조: A=설정키, B=설정값, C=설명, D=사용여부, E=최종수정일시
  // 연결 식별자/원본 보존 설정은 과거 FALSE가 남아 있어도 값 자체는 읽습니다.
  // 실제 기능 토글만 사용여부 FALSE를 존중합니다.
  const values = sheet.getRange(3, 1, lastRow - 2, Math.max(5, sheet.getLastColumn())).getValues();
  const config = {};
  const alwaysReadKeys = new Set([
    'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID',
    'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET',
    'MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID',
    'STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID',
    'TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID', // 하위호환: 학생마스터 읽기 전용 참조
    'STUDENT_MASTER_SHEET',
    'DORM_MASTER_SHEET',
    'TIMEZONE',
    'PROCESS_MODE',
    'STRUCTURE_VERSION',
  ]);

  values.forEach((r) => {
    const key = text_(r[0]);
    if (!key) return;

    const value = r[1];
    const enabledRaw = r[3];
    const enabledText = String(enabledRaw ?? '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const explicitlyDisabled =
      enabledRaw === false ||
      enabledText === 'FALSE' ||
      enabledText === 'N' ||
      enabledText === 'NO';

    if (alwaysReadKeys.has(key) || !explicitlyDisabled) {
      config[key] = value;
    }
  });

  return config;
}
function setConfigValue_(key, value) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.CONFIG_SHEET);
  const lastRow = Math.max(sheet.getLastRow(), 3);
  const keys = sheet.getRange(3, 1, lastRow - 2, 1).getValues().flat().map(text_);
  const index = keys.indexOf(key);
  if (index >= 0) {
    sheet.getRange(index + 3, 2).setValue(value);
    sheet.getRange(index + 3, 5).setValue(new Date()).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  } else {
    sheet.appendRow([key, value, '스크립트 자동 생성 설정', true, new Date()]);
  }
}

function requiredConfig_(config, key) {
  const value = text_(config[key]);
  if (!value) throw new Error(`00_처리설정의 ${key} 값이 없습니다.`);
  return value;
}

    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.CONFIG_SHEET,

  ].forEach((name) => {
    if (!ss.getSheetByName(name)) throw new Error(`필수 탭 누락: ${name}`);
  });
}

function showUepDormOutingProcessorStatus() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive();
  const normalized = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const daily = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const errors = ss.getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET).getLastRow() - 2;
  const triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers()
    .filter((t) => t.getHandlerFunction() === UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER).length;

  const masterSpreadsheetId =
    text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    text_(config.TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    'UEP 학사외출 처리 상태',
    [
      `스크립트 버전: ${UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.VERSION}`,
      `설치상태: ${config.SCRIPT_STATUS || '미설치'}`,
      `자동 트리거: ${triggers}개`,
      `정규화 영구원장: ${Math.max(0, normalized)}건`,
      `일자별·규칙판정: ${Math.max(0, daily)}건`,
      `오류검토: ${Math.max(0, errors)}건`,
      `원본 읽기: ${config.SOURCE_READ_MODE || 'FORM_DIRECT'}`,
      `현재 폼 응답 수: ${config.LAST_SOURCE_FORM_SYNC_COUNT || '0'}`,
      `학생/학사생 참조: ${masterSpreadsheetId}`,
      `처리경로: FORM → 정규화 → 일자별 → UEP 직접조회`,
      `중복정책: 동일 논리신청 최신 제출 우선`,
      `기본정보 재전송: DISABLED`,
      `최근 성공: ${config.LAST_SUCCESS_AT || '-'}`,
      `최근 메일: ${config.LAST_DORM_MAIL_AT || '-'}`,
    ].join('\n'),
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

function rowToObject_(headers, row) {
  const obj = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (h) obj[h] = row[i];
  });
  return obj;
}

function firstValue_(obj, keys) {
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
    const value = obj[key];
    if (value !== '' && value !== null && typeof value !== 'undefined') return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function firstDate_(obj, keys) {
  return toDateOnly_(firstValue_(obj, keys));
}

function firstTime_(obj, keys) {
  return formatTime_(firstValue_(obj, keys));
}

function firstValue_(obj, keys) {
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;

  }

function parseIdentity_(value) {
  const match = text_(value).match(/^(\d+)반\s+(\d+)번\s+(.+)$/);
  if (!match) return null;
  return { classNo: match[1], studentNo: match[2], name: match[3].trim() };
}

function buildResponseId_(timestamp, identity, type, category, date, rowNumber) {
  const base = [
    formatDateTime_(timestamp),
    text_(identity),
    type,
    category,
    formatDate_(date),
    rowNumber,
  ].join('|');
  return `FRESP-${shortHash_(base)}-${rowNumber}`;
}

function shortHash_(value) {
  const bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    String(value),
    Utilities.Charset.UTF_8
  );
  return bytes.slice(0, 8)
    .map((b) => ((b + 256) % 256).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
}

function mergeNotes_(oldNote, newNote) {
  const parts = String(oldNote || '').split(' | ').filter(Boolean);
  if (!parts.includes(newNote)) parts.push(newNote);
  return parts.join(' | ');
}

  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.j

function installUepDormOutingAutoTrigger() {
  const config = getConfig_();
  const interval = Number(config.AUTO_INTERVAL_MINUTES || 5);
  const validIntervals = [1, 5, 10, 15, 30];
  const minutes = validIntervals.includes(interval) ? interval : 5;

  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach((trigger) => {
    if (trigger.getHandlerFunction() === UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER) {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);
    }
  });

  ScriptApp.newTrigger(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.AUTO_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .everyMinutes(minutes)
    .create();

  setConfigValue_('AUTO_TRIGGER_STATUS', `INSTALLED_${minutes}MIN`);
  SpreadsheetApp.getActive().toast(
    `${minutes}분 간격 자동 처리 트리거를 설치했습니다.`,
    UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MENU,
    7
  );
}

function firstValue_(obj, keys) {
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
    const value = obj[key];
    if (value !== '' && value !== null && typeof value !== 'undefined') return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function firstValue_(obj, keys) {
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) continue;
    const value = obj[key];
    if (value !== '' && value !== null && typeof value !== 'undefined') return value;
  }

function firstDate_(obj, keys) {
  return toDateOnly_(firstValue_(obj, keys));

function firstTime_(obj, keys) {
  return formatTime_(firstValue_(obj, keys));
}

function parseIdentity_(value) {
  const match = text_(value).match(/^(\d+)반\s+(\d+)번\s+(.+)$/);
  if (!match) return null;
  return { classNo: match[1], studentNo: match[2], name: match[3].trim() };
}

function buildResponseId_(timestamp, identity, type, category, date, rowNumber) {
  const base = [
    formatDateTime_(timestamp),
    text_(identity),
    type,
    category,
    formatDate_(date),
    rowNumber,
  ].join('|');
  return `FRESP-${shortHash_(base)}-${rowNumber}`;
}

function shortHash_(value) {
  const bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    String(value),
    Utilities.Charset.UTF_8
  );
  return bytes.slice(0, 8)
    .map((b) => ((b + 256) % 256).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
}

function mergeNotes_(oldNote, newNote) {
  const parts = String(oldNote || '').split(' | ').filter(Boolean);
  if (!parts.includes(newNote)) parts.push(newNote);
  return parts.join(' | ');
}

  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.j

async function fetchComciganTimetable(options={}){
  const schoolCode=String(options.comciganSchoolCode||"69092").trim();
  const targetDate=String(options.date||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10));
  try{
    const first=await fetchComciganRaw(schoolCode,1);
    const selected=selectComciganPeriod(first?.periods,targetDate);
    const raw=selected===1?first:await fetchComciganRaw(schoolCode,selected);
    const timetable=normalizeComciganTimetable(raw,targetDate,options.grade||"1",options.classNo||"1");
    const payload={ok:true,schoolCode,targetDate,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString(),raw,timetable};
    await fs.writeFile(comciganCachePath(),JSON.stringify(payload,null,2),"utf8");
    return payload;
  }catch(error){
    try{const cached=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(comciganCachePath(),"utf8"));return {...cached,offline:true,staleReason:error?.message||String(error)};}catch{}
    throw error;
  }
}

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const key = recoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const payload = {
    version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
    salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
  };
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
  let encrypted = null;
  try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
      try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
    }
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
      const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
      friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
      friendly.setupRequired = true;
      throw friendly;
    }
    throw readError;
  }
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
  }
  // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.

    const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
    if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, leg

vered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

function schoolReadError(message,code='UEP_SCHOOL_READ_ERROR'){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadSchoolReadApiUrl(){
  if(schoolReadPolicyCache.url&&schoolReadPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now())return schoolReadPolicyCache.url;
  let response;
  try{response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:'no-store'});}catch(error){throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');}
  if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정 조회 실패 ('+response.status+')','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');
  const policy=await response.json();
  const url=String(policy?.schoolReadApiUrl||UEP_SCHOOL_READ_API_FALLBACK||'').trim();
  if(!/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[^/]+\/exec(?:\?.*)?$/i.test(url))throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 배포 URL이 아직 등록되지 않았습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  schoolReadPolicyCache={url,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return url;
}

function decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(payload){
  if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=='aes-256-gcm')throw new Error('invalid OAuth recovery token');
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,'base64'),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,'base64'),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,'base64'),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,'base64');
  const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm',googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString('utf8'));
}

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);

  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);

e(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];

  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}


function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
  let sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){
    const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
    if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
  }else{
    let max=0;
    rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
    id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
  }
  const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,id,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

async function deleteOfficialAttendance(id="") {
  const key=String(id||"").trim();
  if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000"]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

async function readInputCenterAsset(fileValue=''){const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue);if(!filePath)return {ok:false,reason:'원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};const ext=path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();const mime={'.jpg':'image/jpeg','.jpeg':'image/jpeg','.png':'image/png','.webp':'image/webp','.gif':'image/gif'}[ext];if(!mime)return {ok:false,reason:'화면 미리보기는 JPG·PNG·WEBP 이미지를 지원합니다.',path:filePath};const data=await fs.readFile(filePath);return {ok:true,path:filePath,name:path.basename(filePath),dataUrl:`${'data:'}${mime};base64,${data.toString('base64')}`};}
async function openInputCenterAsset(fileValue=''){const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue);if(!filePath)return {ok:false,reason:'원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};const reason=await shell.openPath(filePath);return {ok:!reason,reason,path:filePath};}

function writeState(value) {
  // Serialize renderer saves so rapid UI updates cannot race each other.
  const task = stateWriteChain.then(() => writeStateNow(value));
  stateWriteChain = task.catch(() => {});
  return task;
}

async function boardNoticeDelete08293(payload={}){
  const ctx=await boardWriteContext08291();ctx.displayAuthor=boardDisplayAuthor08293(ctx,payload);const sheet=String(payload.sheet||''),noticeId=String(payload.noticeId||'').trim();
  if(!['06_1학년공지','07_2학년공지','08_전교공지'].includes(sheet)||!noticeId)throw boardWriteError08291('공지 삭제 대상을 확인해 주세요.','UEP_BOARD_NOTICE_DELETE_INVALID');
  const rows=await boardNoticeRows08293(ctx),item=rows.find(x=>x.sheet===sheet&&x.noticeId===noticeId);if(!item||!item.canDelete)throw boardWriteError08291('이 공지를 삭제할 권한이 없습니다.','UEP_BOARD_NOTICE_DELETE_FORBIDDEN');
  const now=boardNow08291();await boardUpdateValues08293(ctx.token,[{range:"'"+sheet+"'!J"+item.rowNumber,values:[['N']]},{range:"'"+sheet+"'!M"+item.rowNumber,values:[[now]]}]);
  const check=await boardReadRange08291(ctx.token,"'"+sheet+"'!A"+item.rowNumber+":M"+item.rowNumber);if(String(check?.[0]?.[9]||'').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N')throw boardWriteError08291('공지 삭제 상태 재확인에 실패했습니다.','UEP_BOARD_NOTICE_DELETE_VERIFY');
  await boardAudit08291(ctx,'공지삭제','SUCCESS',noticeId+' / '+item.target,noticeId);return {ok:true,noticeId,sheet,verified:true};
}
function createWindow() {
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1500, height: 960, minWidth: 1050, minHeight: 700,
    backgroundColor: "#f4f8fd", title: "UNHO Education Platform (UEP)", frame: false,
    icon: path.join(__dirname, "..", "assets", "unho-uep-icon.png"),
    webPreferences: { preload: path.join(__dirname, "preload.cjs"), contextIsolation: true, nodeIntegration: false },
  });
  mainWindow = win;
  win.setMenuBarVisibility(false);
  win.on("close", () => {
    // A hidden Comcigan BrowserWindow used to keep Electron alive after the main
    // UI disappeared. Destroy auxiliary windows before the last visible window closes.
    destroyAuxiliaryWindows("main-window-close");
  });
  win.on("closed", () => {
    mainWindow = null;
    beginCleanShutdown("main-window-closed");
  });
  win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, "..", "index.html"));
}
